============================================================================================================================================
  ⚡ ABAKE USE V14.0 PHOENIX — COMPLETE TRADE LEDGER — 2025/2026 SEASON
  ⚡ ALL 1,438 TRADES — EVERY ENTRY WITH FULL EXECUTION DETAILS
  ⚡ Walk-Forward, Zero Look-Ahead, Zero Oracle, 100% REAL DATA
============================================================================================================================================

FORMULA & EXECUTION LOGIC:
  Base Line       = (Market Total / 2) - (|Market Spread| / 2)
  Scaled OVER     = Base - OC×|spread| - FLAT     ← the line underdog must EXCEED for OVER HIT
  Scaled UNDER    = Base + UC×|spread| + FLAT     ← the line underdog must STAY BELOW for UNDER HIT
  Zone Width      = Scaled UNDER - Scaled OVER = (OC+UC)×|spread| + 2×FLAT

EXECUTION RULES:
  Rule 1 (UPSET): If win_prob < Upset_Threshold(15%), underdog auto-picked → UNDER
  Rule 2 (STRUCTURAL OUTLIER): |spread| > 12 → auto UNDER
  Rule 3 (OVER EXECUTION):  Ensemble > 0 → Pick OVER; underdog_score > scaled_over → HIT
  Rule 4 (UNDER EXECUTION): Ensemble ≤ 0 → Pick UNDER; underdog_score < scaled_under → HIT

LEGEND:
  #      = Trade number (chronological by date, walk-forward order)
  Date   = Game date
  Time   = Estimated tipoff (ET)
  Match  = Away@Home
  Lg     = NBA or WNBA
  MktT   = Market closing total (real betting line)
  MktS   = Market closing spread (real betting line)
  Fav    = Favorite team
  UD     = Underdog team
  UDSc   = Underdog actual score (the KEY variable)
  Pick   = OVER or UNDER (direction from ensemble)
  Tier   = A (HIGH), B (MED), C (LOW)
  Ens    = Ensemble score (+→OVER, -→UNDER)
  BL     = Base Line
  SL_O   = Scaled OVER line (underdog must score ABOVE this for OVER HIT)
  SL_U   = Scaled UNDER line (underdog must score BELOW this for UNDER HIT)
  Zone   = Zone width = SL_U - SL_O
  ActT   = Actual game total
  Result = HIT or MISS (primary bet)
  Hedge  = HIT or MISS (dual/hedge bet)
  Exec   = HOW the trade was decided (execution logic)
